<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion exchange with prophetic report. Attribution: Al-Harith ibn Abdullah ibn Aws narrating his exchange with Umar. Read the entire question about a menstruating pilgrim, Umar&#x27;s answer, Al-Harith&#x27;s attribution to the Prophet and Umar&#x27;s rebuke. Preserve the source&#x27;s own legal wording without reconciling it to another report.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Al-Harith ibn Abdullah ibn Aws said:I came to Umar ibn al-Khattab and asked him about a woman who has performed the (obligatory) circumambulation on the day of sacrifice, and then she menstruates. He said: She must perform the last circumambulation of the House (the Ka&#x27;bah). Al-Harith said: The Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) told me the same thing. Umar said: May your hands fall down! You asked me about a thing that you had asked the Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) so that I might oppose him</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2004]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2004" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 2004</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   2004 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion exchange with prophetic report. Attribution: Al-Harith ibn Abdullah ibn Aws narrating his exchange with Umar. Read the full Hindi dialogue and its supplied parenthetical glosses; the final rebuke belongs to Umar, and the earlier answer is reported by Al-Harith as also given by the Prophet. Retain attribution and wording as supplied.</p>

<p dir="auto">हारिस बिन अब्दुल्लाह बिन औस कहते हैं कि मैं उमर बिन ख़त्ताब (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास आया और आप से उस औरत के मुतल्लिक़ पूछा जो यौम-उन-नहर को बैतुल्लाह का तवाफ़ (इफ़ा़दह) कर चुकी हो, फिर उसे हैज़ आ गया हो? उन्होंने कहा: वो आख़िरी तवाफ़ (तवाफ़-ए-विदा) कर के जाए (यानी: तवाफ़-ए-विदा का इंतज़ार करे), हारिस ने कहा: इसी तरह मुझे रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने भी बताया था, इस पर उमर ने कहा तेरे दोनों हाथ गिर जाएं! तुम ने मुझ से ऐसी बात पूछी जिसय तुम रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से पूछ चुके थे ताके मैं इस के ख़िलाफ़ बयान करूं।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2004]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2004" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حارث بن عبداللہ بن اوس کہتے ہیں کہ میں عمر بن خطاب رضی اللہ عنہ کے پاس آیا اور آپ سے اس عورت کے متعلق پوچھا جو یوم النحر کو بیت اللہ کا طواف ( افاضہ ) کر چکی ہو، پھر اسے حیض آ گیا ہو؟ انہوں نے کہا: وہ آخری طواف ( طواف وداع ) کر کے جائے ( یعنی: طواف وداع کا انتظار کرے ) ، حارث نے کہا: اسی طرح مجھے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے بھی بتایا تھا، اس پر عمر نے کہا تیرے دونوں ہاتھ گر جائیں ۱؎! تم نے مجھ سے ایسی بات پوچھی جسے تم رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے پوچھ چکے تھے تاکہ میں اس کے خلاف بیان کروں۔</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2004]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2004" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Harith bin Abdullah bin Aus kehte hain ki main Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas aaya aur aap se us aurat ke mutaliq poocha jo yaum-un-nahr ko Baitullah ka tawaf (ifadah) kar chuki ho, phir usay haiz aa gaya ho? Unhon ne kaha: Woh aakhri tawaf (tawaf-e-wida) kar ke jaye (yani: tawaf-e-wida ka intezar kare), Harith ne kaha: Isi tarah mujhe Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne bhi bataya tha, is par Umar ne kaha tere dono haath gir jayen! Tum ne mujh se aisi baat poochhi jisay tum Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se pooch chuke thay taake main is ke khilaf bayan karoon.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2004" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 11</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2005]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2005" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 2005</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   2005 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا وَهْبُ بْنُ بَقِيَّةَ، عَنْ خَالِدٍ، عَنْ أَفْلَحَ، عَنِ الْقَاسِمِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، - رضى الله عنها - قَالَتْ أَحْرَمْتُ مِنَ التَّنْعِيمِ بِعُمْرَةٍ فَدَخَلْتُ فَقَضَيْتُ عُمْرَتِي وَانْتَظَرَنِي رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِالأَبْطَحِ حَتَّى فَرَغْتُ وَأَمَرَ النَّاسَ بِالرَّحِيلِ ‏.‏ قَالَتْ وَأَتَى رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم الْبَيْتَ فَطَافَ بِهِ ثُمَّ خَرَجَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2005" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report with prophetic action. Attribution: Aisha describing her Tan&#x27;im umrah and the Prophet&#x27;s departure. Read the complete first-person account, the supplied English atonement wording, the wait at Abtah, departure command and final circumambulation. Do not import absent wording into Hindi.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Aisha, Ummul Mu&#x27;minin: I put on ihram for umrah at at-Tan&#x27;im and I entered (Mecca) and performed my umrah as an atonement. The Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) waited for me at al-Abtah till I finished it. He commanded the people to depart. The Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) came to the House (the Ka&#x27;bah), went round it and went out (i.e. left for Medina)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2005]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2005" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report with prophetic action. Attribution: Aisha describing her Tan&#x27;im umrah and the Prophet&#x27;s departure. Read the full Hindi account independently; select exactly its own umrah, waiting, departure and circumambulation wording, including source parentheticals.</p>

<p dir="auto">उम्म-उल-मोमिनीन आइशा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हा) कहती हैं कि मैं ने तन्&#x27;ईम से उमराह का एहराम बांधा फिर मैं (मक्काह) गई और अपना उमराह पूरा किया, रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने मक़ाम अबतह में मेरा इंतज़ार किया यहां तक कि मैं फ़ारिग़ हो कर (आप के पास वापस आ गई) तो आप ने लोगों को रवानगी का हुक्म दिया, आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) खाना का&#x27;आबा आए और इस का तवाफ़ किया फिर रवाना हुए।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2005]</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ام المؤمنین عائشہ رضی اللہ عنہا کہتی ہیں کہ میں نے تنعیم سے عمرے کا احرام باندھا پھر میں ( مکہ ) گئی اور اپنا عمرہ پورا کیا، رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے مقام ابطح ۱؎ میں میرا انتظار کیا یہاں تک کہ میں فارغ ہو کر ( آپ کے پاس واپس آ گئی ) تو آپ نے لوگوں کو روانگی کا حکم دیا، آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم خانہ کعبہ آئے اور اس کا طواف کیا پھر روانہ ہوئے۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2005" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2005]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2005" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Umm-ul-Momineen Aisha (Radi Allahu Anha) kehti hain ki main ne Tan&#x27;eem se umrah ka ehram bandha phir main (Makkah) gayi aur apna umrah poora kiya, Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne maqam Abtah mein mera intezar kiya yahan tak ki main farigh ho kar (Aap ke paas wapas aa gayi) toh Aap ne logon ko rawaangi ka hukm diya, Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) khana ka&#x27;aba aaye aur is ka tawaf kiya phir rawaana huye.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2005" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>
